Fallback: \"the answer is X\" / \"The answer is X\" (case insensitive)\n    # 3. Fallback: \"= X\" 在文本末尾附近（最后 200 字符内）\n    # 4. 最后手段: 文本最后一行的数字\n    ...\n```\n\n注意：\n- 先读现有代码理解 answer extraction 的位置和接口\n- fallback 只在 `\\boxed{}` 匹配失败时触发\n- 提取的答案需要 strip 空白和句号\n- 用 regex 实现，import re（如果没有的话）\n\n### 改动 3: 记录 finish_reason\n\n在 trace 生成阶段，vLLM API 返回的 response 中应包含 `finish_reason` 字段（\"stop\" 或 \"length\"）。修改 trace_generator.py：\n- 在每条 trace 生成后记录 finish_reason\n- 将 finish_reason 保存到 trace 数据结构中（添加字段）\n- 在 run_full_mvp.py 的 per-problem 结果中汇总 truncated_count（finish_reason=\"length\" 的数量）\n\n### 改动 4: --save-intermediates 选项\n\n文件：`/root/symb_invariant_consensus/run_full_mvp.py`\n\n添加 `--save-intermediates` CLI flag：\n- 开启后，每个问题的结果 JSON 中保留 `raw_constraints`（提取的原始约束列表）和 `z3_parse_results`（每个约束的 parse 成功/失败及 z3 表达式）\n- 默认关闭（保持与 v3 行为兼容）\n- 在 `argparse` 中添加 `--save-intermediates` action='store_true'\n- 找到当前 strip 这些字段的代码位置，加条件判断\n\n### 验证步骤（不启动 pipeline）\n\n1. `python -c \"from sica.trace_generator import ...; print('import OK')\"` 验证 import 无语法错误\n2. `python -c \"from sica.trace_generator import extract_answer; assert extract_answer(r'\\boxed{42}') == '42'; assert extract_answer('The answer is 127.') == '127'; assert extract_answer('Therefore x = 902') == '902'; print('ALL PASS')\"` 验证 fallback extraction\n3. `python run_full_mvp.py --help` 验证 --save-intermediates 参数存在\n4. `python -c \"import ast; ast.parse(open('sica/trace_generator.py').read()); ast.parse(open('run_full_mvp.py').read()); print('syntax OK')\"` 验证语法\n\n### 产出\n\n完成后汇报：\n1. 每个文件的具体改动摘要（行号 + 改了什么）\n2. 验证步骤的输出\n3. 是否发现其他需要关注的问题"

## 任务：SICA v4 代码修复（4 项改动）

**服务器**: westd-32608, root 用户
**项目路径**: /root/symb_invariant_consensus/
**Python 环境**: conda base (`source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base`)
**⚠️ 硬约束**: v3 实验正在运行 (PID 129992)，**禁止重启 vLLM**，**禁止运行任何会向 vLLM 发请求的测试**。所有修改只改代码文件，不执行 pipeline。

### 背景

v2 结果中 P8/P9/P11 因 max_tokens=10240 截断导致模型未输出 `\boxed{}`，12/12 trace 全部 0 答案。P2 因 Z3 崩溃导致 traces 被清空（代码已修复）。v4 将用修复后的代码重跑这些截断题。

### 改动 1: max_tokens 10240 → 16384

文件：`/root/symb_invariant_consensus/sica/trace_generator.py`

找到 max_tokens 参数（应该在 vLLM API 调用处），将默认值从 10240 改为 16384。也检查 `run_full_mvp.py` 中是否有 `--max-tokens` 参数传入，如有也改默认值。

### 改动 2: Fallback answer extraction

文件：`/root/symb_invariant_consensus/sica/trace_generator.py`（或 answer extraction 所在文件）

当前答案提取只匹配 `\boxed{...}` 模式。添加 fallback 逻辑：

```python
def extract_answer(text: str) -> str | None:
    # 1. 优先: \boxed{...}（已有逻辑）
    # 2. Fallback: "the answer is X" / "The answer is X" (case insensitive)
    # 3. Fallback: "= X" 在文本末尾附近（最后 200 字符内）
    # 4. 最后手段: 文本最后一行的数字
    ...
```

注意：
- 先读现有代码理解 answer extraction 的位置和接口
- fallback 只在 `\boxed{}` 匹配失败时触发
- 提取的答案需要 strip 空白和句号
- 用 regex 实现，import re（如果没有的话）

### 改动 3: 记录 finish_reason

在 trace 生成阶段，vLLM API 返回的 response 中应包含 `finish_reason` 字段（"stop" 或 "length"）。修改 trace_generator.py：
- 在每条 trace 生成后记录 finish_reason
- 将 finish_reason 保存到 trace 数据结构中（添加字段）
- 在 run_full_mvp.py 的 per-problem 结果中汇总 truncated_count（finish_reason="length" 的数量）

### 改动 4: --save-intermediates 选项

文件：`/root/symb_invariant_consensus/run_full_mvp.py`

添加 `--save-intermediates` CLI flag：
- 开启后，每个问题的结果 JSON 中保留 `raw_constraints`（提取的原始约束列表）和 `z3_parse_results`（每个约束的 parse 成功/失败及 z3 表达式）
- 默认关闭（保持与 v3 行为兼容）
- 在 `argparse` 中添加 `--save-intermediates` action='store_true'
- 找到当前 strip 这些字段的代码位置，加条件判断

### 验证步骤（不启动 pipeline）

1. `python -c "from sica.trace_generator import ...; print('import OK')"` 验证 import 无语法错误
2. `python -c "from sica.trace_generator import extract_answer; assert extract_answer(r'\boxed{42}') == '42'; assert extract_answer('The answer is 127.') == '127'; assert extract_answer('Therefore x = 902') == '902'; print('ALL PASS')"` 验证 fallback extraction
3. `python run_full_mvp.py --help` 验证 --save-intermediates 参数存在
4. `python -c "import ast; ast.parse(open('sica/trace_generator.py').read()); ast.parse(open('run_full_mvp.py').read()); print('syntax OK')"` 验证语法

### 产出

完成后汇报：
1. 每个文件的具体改动摘要（行号 + 改了什么）
2. 验证步骤的输出
3. 是否发现其他需要关注的问题

Show all your reasoning.\\nAt the end, put your final answer in \\\\\\\\boxed{{}}.\\n\\nProblem: {problem}\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n\\ndef extract_boxed_answer(text: str) -> str:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Extract the last \\\\\\\\boxed{...} content from text, handling nested braces.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    idx = text.rfind('\\\\\\\\boxed{')\\n    if idx == -1:\\n        return \\\"\\\"\\n    start = idx + len('\\\\\\\\boxed{')\\n    depth = 1\\n    i = start\\n    while i < len(text) and depth > 0:\\n        if text[i] == '{':\\n            depth += 1\\n        elif text[i] == '}':\\n            depth -= 1\\n        i += 1\\n    return text[start:i-1].strip()\\n\\n\\ndef retry_with_backoff(func, max_retries=3, base_delay=1.0):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Simple exponential backoff retry.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    for attempt in range(max_retries + 1):\\n        try:\\n            return func()\\n        except Exception as e:\\n            if attempt == max_retries:\\n                raise\\n            delay = base_delay * (2 ** attempt) + random.uniform(0, 0.5)\\n            logger.warning(\\\"Retry %d/%d after %.1fs: %s\\\", attempt + 1, max_retries, delay, e)\\n            time.sleep(delay)\\n\\n\\nclass TraceGenerator(ABC):\\n    @abstractmethod\\n    def generate(self, problem: str, k: int = 12) -> list[dict]:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Generate K reasoning traces. Prefers n=K batch, falls back to async concurrency.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(self, base_url: str = \\\"http://localhost:8000/v1\\\",\\n                 api_key: [REDACTED] = \\\"EMPTY\\\", model: str | None = None,\\n                 temperature: float = 0.7, top_p: float = 0.95,\\n                 max_tokens: int = 4096, seed: int | None = None):\\n        import openai\\n        self.client = openai.OpenAI(base_url=base_url, api_key=[REDACTED], timeout=600.0)\\n        self.async_client = openai.AsyncOpenAI(base_url=base_url, api_key=[REDACTED], timeout=600.0)\\n        self.temperature = temperature\\n        self.top_p = top_p\\n        self.max_tokens = max_tokens\\n        self.seed = seed\\n        if model:\\n            self.model = model\\n        else:\\n            models = self.client.models.list()\\n            self.model = models.data[0].id\\n            logger.info(\\\"Auto-detected model: %s\\\", self.model)\\n\\n    def _build_prompt(self, problem: str) -> str:\\n        return SOLVE_PROMPT.format(problem=problem)\\n\\n    def _generate_n_batch(self, prompt: str, k: int) -> list[dict]:\\n        batch_size = 4\\n        results = []\\n        for batch_start in range(0, k, batch_size):\\n            batch_n = min(batch_size, k - batch_start)\\n            logger.info(\\\"Generating traces %d-%d of %d\\\", batch_start + 1, batch_start + batch_n, k)\\n            response = retry_with_backoff(lambda _n=batch_n: self.client.chat.completions.create(\\n                model=self.model,\\n                messages=[{\\\"role\\\": \\\"user\\\", \\\"content\\\": prompt}],\\n                temperature=self.temperature,\\n                top_p=self.top_p,\\n                max_tokens=self.max_tokens,\\n                n=_n,\\n                **({\\\"seed\\\": self.seed} if self.seed is not None else {}),\\n            ))\\n            for i, choice in enumerate(response.choices):\\n                text = choice.message.content or \\\"\\\"\\n                answer = extract_boxed_answer(text)\\n                results.append({\\\"trace\\\": text, \\\"answer\\\": answer, \\\"trace_idx\\\": batch_start + i})\\n        return results\\n\\n    async def _generate_single(self, prompt: str, trace_idx: int) -> dict:\\n        response = await self.async_client.chat.completions.create(\\n            model=self.model,\\n            messages=\\n\\n... problems.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        results = []\\n        for i, p in enumerate(problems):\\n            logger.info(\\\"Processing problem %d/%d\\\", i + 1, len(problems))\\n            result = ind = z3.Bool(f\\\"__ind_{i}\\\")\\n            indicators.append(ind)\\n            try:\\n                if not z3.is_bool(uc.z3_formula):\\n                    active_mask.append(False)\\n return MaxSATResult(satisfied=[], excluded=list(unique_constraints),\\n                                total_weight=0, solve_time_ms=0.0)\\n\\n        t0 = time.perf_counter()\\n        result = opt.check()\\n        solve_time = (time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1000\\n\\n        satisfied = []\\n        excluded = []\\n\\n        if result in (z3.sat, z3.unknown):\\n            model = opt.model()\\n            for i, uc in enumerate(unique_constraints):\\n                if not active_mask[i]:\\n                    excluded.append(uc)\\n                    continue\\n                val = model.evaluate(indicators[i], model_completion=True)\\n                if z3.is_true(val):\\n                    satisfied.append(uc)\\n                else:\\n                    excluded.append(uc)\\n        else:\\n            excluded = list(unique_constraints)\\n\\n        total_w = sum(uc.weight for uc in satisfied)\\n        return MaxSATResult(\\n            satisfied=satisfied,\\n            excluded=excluded,\\n            total_weight=total_w,\\n            solve_time_ms=solve_time,\\n        )\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": Show all your reasoning.\\nAt the end, put your final answer in \\\\\\\\boxed{{}}.\\n\\nProblem: {problem}\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n\\ndef extract_boxed_answer(text: str) -> str:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Extract the last \\\\\\\\boxed{...} content from text, handling nested braces.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    idx = text.rfind('\\\\\\\\boxed{')\\n    if idx == -1:\\n        return \\\"\\\"\\n    start = idx + len('\\\\\\\\boxed{')\\n    depth = 1\\n    i = start\\n    while i < len(text) and depth > 0:\\n        if text[i] == '{':\\n            depth += 1\\n        elif text[i] == '}':\\n            depth -= 1\\n        i += 1\\n    return text[start:i-1].strip()\\n\\n\\ndef extract_answer_fallback(text: str) -> Optional[str]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Fallback answer extraction when \\\\\\\\boxed{} is missing (e.g., truncated traces).\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    patterns = [\\n        # \\\"the answer is X\\\" / \\\"the final answer is X\\\"\\n        r'(?:the\\\\s+)?(?:final\\\\s+)?answer\\\\s+is\\\\s*[:\\\\s]*\\\\$?\\\\\\\\?boxed\\\\{([^}]+)\\\\}',\\n        r'(?:the\\\\s+)?(?:final\\\\s+)?answer\\\\s+is\\\\s*[:\\\\s]*\\\\$?\\\\*?\\\\*?([+-]?\\\\d+(?:\\\\.\\\\d+)?)\\\\*?\\\\*?\\\\$?',\\n        # \\\"therefore/thus/hence ... = X\\\"\\n        r'(?:therefore|thus|hence|so)\\\\s*,?\\\\s*[^=\\\\n]{0,80}?=\\\\s*\\\\$?\\\\*?\\\\*?([+-]?\\\\d+(?:\\\\.\\\\d+)?)\\\\*?\\\\*?\\\\$?',\\n        # \\\"= X\\\" near end (last 500 chars)\\n        r'=\\\\s*\\\\$?\\\\*?\\\\*?([+-]?\\\\d+(?:\\\\.\\\\d+)?)\\\\*?\\\\*?\\\\$?\\\\s*$',\\n    ]\\n\\n    # Try last 1000 chars first (final answer more likely near end), then full text\\n    for search_text in [text[-1000:], text]:\\n        for pattern in patterns:\\n            matches = re.findall(pattern, search_text, re.IGNORECASE | re.MULTILINE)\\n            if matches:\\n                return matches[-1].strip()\\n    return None\\n\\n\\ndef extract_answer(text: str) -> tuple[str, str]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    Extract answer from trace text.
